= Appendix: Requirements

Completion-program note: the confirmed non-Gherkin `v0.7.x` rows in this appendix are release-scope requirements derived from `docs/requirements/v0.7.x-completion-contract.md`. They remain explicitly planned or open except for the locally resolved P0 build-scaffold admission gate, and they do not alter the configured feature inventory, Gherkin tags, implementation statuses, or totals below. The scaffold receipt promotes no production capability status.

Status: implementation-informed for separately bounded implemented slices and for the current atomic CATALOG-001/CATALOG-002/RESULT-001 Kotlin CLI behavior; broader coverage and qualification need review. CATALOG-001, CATALOG-002, and RESULT-001 are declared `@implemented-and-validated` within separately bounded source-built evidence. The configured `features/` root contains eleven feature documents, 40 repository-unique requirement definitions, 85 expanded executable cases, 697 declared scenario steps, two backgrounds with 10 background steps, and 1,291 expanded case steps. All 40 definitions/85 cases are effectively `@implemented-and-validated` within their owning evidence boundaries; REQ-JAVA-CLI-RESULT-001 contributes one dual-classified definition/case and 30 declared/expanded steps. It is dated mutable aggregate evidence and does not qualify the selector, packaged or cross-platform execution, concurrent rollback, crash/restart, general rollback authority, numerical coverage, or altered `PatchEngine` authority.

The external research and target-scoped concepts above were reviewed against RefactorKit base commit `c1d70fc51ef84fa25baaab5678dece69f8ce739a`; bounded implementation claims rely separately on dated mutable working-tree evidence. The 2026-07-30 clarification defines full-reactor graph completeness as descriptor-closed over selected nodes: every active reactor POM remains mandatory, every selected external node needs complete effective descriptor closure, and every outgoing declaration/classification is hash-bound. Bounded `010` validates the prune/alternate-selected-path boundary; bounded `011` independently validates the ordinary sidecar-free selected-`jar` row and its three under-lock drift gates; and six-case dual-classified outline `012` validates fail-closed `MISSING`, `MALFORMED`, and `DRIFTED` selected-descriptor outcomes with parsed relocation/model identity proof and stable recorded evidence. The follow-up Magrathea field observation is separately implementation-informed for consumer commit `a48e3c06` through the source-built CLI on Linux/JDK 21 after `010`; it is not architecture research promoted into a requirement, did not require `011` or `012`, and does not establish integration/native-matrix or general qualification.

The LATE REQ-JAVA-MAVEN-MOVE-AUTH-003 review reconciles the corrected feature with actual implementation. Four isolated copies use the ordinary default scanner to exercise a readable source omitted after an effective primary Maven `testSourceDirectory` replacement, a readable system-path JAR whose strict evidence-manifest fingerprint differs, a recovered target use without syntax failure, and a materialized generated-root inventory mismatch owned by `catalog-generated-support:main`. The effective model keeps primary main/test directories separate from additive build-helper/Kotlin roots: replacing a primary directory removes only the corresponding default Java root, while additive declarations retain defaults, and no plugin is executed. The two strict format-version-1 expected inventory manifests are snapshot-bound auxiliary files; blocker evidence carries manifest content hashes, and the classpath row carries expected/observed artifact fingerprints. Repeat guidance values and JSON are deterministic and schema/checklist version 1; CLI `--apply` refuses as `guidance.nonManaged` before `PatchEngine`, lock, or WAL. On 2026-07-30, the independent final OpenJDK 21 `./gradlew --no-daemon --rerun-tasks check goldenTest` run reported `BUILD SUCCESSFUL` in 7m 13s: 956 discovered, 773 executed, 183 skipped/tag-filtered, zero failures/errors, and 77 tasks executed; reviewed log SHA-256: `8d9b19576a27deaae2c12c767f929664c228db85d1dcc8644de3e41ecbe6ca9d`. This is dated mutable working-tree evidence; SpotBugs retained the configured non-blocking baseline findings. The REQ-003 implementation claim remains bounded to the Java dispatcher/source-built CLI; REQ-004 separately owns lexical review, and implemented REQ-008 separately owns its four uncertainty plus two refusal-phase cases.

The LATE REQ-JAVA-MAVEN-MOVE-AUTH-004 review reconciles the promoted feature with current implementation. `JavaMoveClassOperationDispatcher` is the direct Java move-class operation API: `Plan` may carry managed, refused, or result-owned typed structural-refusal results and rejects lexical evidence; `Guidance` carries REQ-003 plus four bounded REQ-008 preview uncertainties; and `LexicalReview` carries REQ-004 alone. This is not generic `LanguageAdapter` direct-apply evidence, and the cross-surface evidence in this paragraph belongs only to REQ-004. Schema-v1 reports `NOT_SEMANTICALLY_PROVEN`/`REVIEW_ONLY`/`LEXICAL_FALLBACK`/`INELIGIBLE`/`evidence.insufficient`, deterministic request/exact-snapshot/hidden-review-artifact/evidence SHA-256 identities, and `lexical-review-<sha256>` non-capability identity. The hidden artifact hash commits to internal compatibility file-operation paths, exact edit ranges, and replacement/create bytes; exposed records are only bounded normalized path/current-content-hash/typed-risk identities with `managedEdit=false`, `COMPLETE or TRUNCATED` record completeness, deterministic returned-category counts, and fixed next actions. CLI/protocol projections contain no plan/edit/range/replacement/apply token; recipe stops without an aggregate; daemon/LSP/MCP use lifecycle-cleared envelope-only LRU correlation; and shared exactly-one apply-identity parsing distinguishes known-operation structured `evidence.insufficient` from unknown/evicted `INVALID_PARAMS`. Daemon/LSP preserve JSON-RPC error `data`; MCP returns structured error content. The dedicated OpenJDK-21 report discovered 22 cases, executed/passed the one 38-step REQ-004 case, skipped 21, and had zero failures/errors; reviewed log SHA-256 `5fb89c290bd098f249c2ec734c8c7bbffae162e5357ab6b1683042fc68e698ce`. The test also preserves clean JDT managed-plan behavior and exact non-mutation. This is source-built/in-process evidence, not packaged/runtime/native qualification.

The LATE REQ-JAVA-MAVEN-MOVE-AUTH-008 review reconciles the corrected feature with current production/test code. Four complete preview uncertainties return immutable schema-v1 `JavaMoveClassReviewOnlyGuidance` with typed layers/codes for inside-closure ambiguity, unresolved static wildcard lookup, outside-closure explicit old FQN with closure precedence, and same-`S0` before/staged diagnostic identity drift; the last is collected by the dispatcher's second stage. None of those four returns `LexicalReview`, plan, or edit, and source-built CLI apply refuses `guidance.nonManaged` pre-`PatchEngine`/lock/WAL/residue. Missing root-declared `catalog-shipping/pom.xml` returns result-owned typed `JavaMoveClassStructuralRefusal`; deterministic component-wise workspace-relative `NOFOLLOW` identity supports a missing module directory and distinguishes it from a missing final POM, while symlink/non-regular states are not `MISSING`; source-built CLI refuses pre-lock/no-residue. The final case starts from eligible REQ-007 and binds one non-managed `BOUND_OTHER` source through immutable `OperationAuthorityFileEvidence`. `PatchEngine` captures one under-lock observed engine snapshot `S1`; immutable `DiagnosticDetails` reports primary `authorityLease.evidenceDrift` plus secondary `snapshot.scopeChanged` before WAL, preserving external `S1` and possibly the lock file. The semantic candidate-inventory hash is expected-only; expected/observed hashes describe required-file evidence, and no observed semantic inventory is claimed. All six remain independent of REQ-003/004/010/011/012. Surface evidence is limited to the source-built library and CLI for the five preview-time rows, and to library/`PatchEngine` acceptance for the under-lock row; no REQ-008 recipe/daemon/LSP/MCP parity is claimed.

== Confirmed `v0.7.x` Completion Contract Requirements (Non-Gherkin)

The following stable IDs decompose the user-confirmed completion program. They are not `.feature` scenarios and are excluded from the 40-definition/85-case Gherkin totals. `Declared status` records the confirmed requirement; `Observed status` never exceeds repository/build evidence.

== Java/Maven Move-Class Apply Authority Epic

This architecture integration summarizes the reviewed requirement without replacing specialist evidence. Accepted ADR 0012 distinguishes global source-set authority from narrower target-scoped Maven `moveClass` authority. The Maven move-class feature contains exactly twelve definitions and 22 cases, all implemented-and-validated. Bounded `001`-`007` and `009`-`012` retain their existing meanings; bounded `008` establishes four immutable preview-guidance outcomes, one active-reactor-POM structural refusal, and one under-lock required-file-evidence refusal. No general or native-matrix authority is inferred. The exact Magrathea `a48e3c06` request remains separately CLI/Linux/JDK-21-qualified. The dedicated packaged-process Cucumber mode is now implemented locally only for existing REQ-001; because it reuses the same feature/ID/pickle, it changes validation mode rather than this catalogue or its counts. The local Linux `amd64` observation does not promote any of the four `CONFIGURED_UNOBSERVED` post-commit native CI rows.

[cols="1,3,2,1",options="header"]
|===
|ID |Slice |Acceptance owner/evidence |Status

|RPK-JAVA-MOVE-001
|First supported result boundary: one uniquely bound top-level class in a writable materialized Maven main/test source set moves within the same module/source set. The direct Java move-class API `JavaMoveClassOperationDispatcher` returns disjoint `Plan(JavaMoveClassPreview)`, `Guidance(JavaMoveClassReviewOnlyGuidance)`, or `LexicalReview(JavaMoveClassLexicalFallbackReviewEnvelope)`. `Plan` may carry managed, refused, or result-owned typed structural-refusal results and rejects lexical evidence; `Guidance` carries REQ-003 plus four bounded REQ-008 preview uncertainties; `LexicalReview` carries REQ-004 alone; and neither non-managed arm can become a core `PatchPlan`. This operation-specific Java boundary is not the generic `LanguageAdapter` `PatchPlan` API. No result silently edits non-Java/string contracts.
|Java adapter contract and operation-shape acceptance; implemented-and-validated REQ-JAVA-MAVEN-MOVE-AUTH-001, `003`, `004`, and `008`: five preview-time library/CLI rows plus one under-lock library/`PatchEngine` row.
|bounded semantic plan; REQ-003 and five REQ-008 preview-time library/CLI outcomes; one REQ-008 library/`PatchEngine` under-lock outcome; REQ-004 direct/CLI/recipe/daemon/LSP/MCP parity; and one local Linux `amd64` packaged-process REQ-001 mode implementation-informed; REQ-002..012 packaged/runtime, general, and every native CI row remain planned P0

|RPK-JAVA-MOVE-002
|Every active reactor POM and, per main/test projection, every selected external node's effective POM/parent/BOM/management/mediation/relocation closure must be complete, current, unambiguous, and hash-bound. Every outgoing declaration has a selected or exact derived-scope/optional/exclusion-pruned record with path/projection provenance. A pruned edge contributes no child/subtree and needs no child POM/JAR on that path; alternate selected paths restore requirements. Selection precedes child lookup when downstream facts cannot affect it; type/classifier are identity, not prune reasons. Ownership, source/materialized-generated-root inventory, Java platform/provider, full-reactor candidate audit/exclusion, and reverse-observer facts remain global. Selected-missing-binary evidence is operation-closure scoped; missing included descriptors block.
|Build Model and JDT reactor acceptance. Bounded `001`/`002` own complete-`AVAILABLE` and observer rows; `006`/`007`/`009`-`012` retain their existing outcomes; `008` adds the root-declared active-POM and closure/candidate/lookup/diagnostic uncertainty cases without changing selected-descriptor boundaries.
|all twelve bounded definitions/22 cases implementation-informed; general, packaged/runtime integration, and native-matrix acceptance remain absent

|RPK-JAVA-MOVE-003
|One exact non-recovered target declaration plus an independent bounded lexical Java inventory of every old-FQN and type-capable matching simple-name range. JDT classifies each candidate exactly once as `BOUND_TARGET`, `BOUND_OTHER`, or `UNRESOLVED`; every target-relevant or relevance-uncertain unresolved candidate blocks. Only `BOUND_TARGET` selects edits. In the ordinary missing-binary row, every target simple-name use is protected by an exact resolved non-static single-type target import, or the use is the exact target FQN; relevant wildcard/static ambiguity, unprotected lookup, recovered/problem evidence, and candidate/diagnostic/edit overlap block. Every retained diagnostic needs exact unchanged mapped before/staged identity and positive non-concealment proof; staged analysis preserves other-symbol classifications, resolves the moved target identity, and introduces no new/changed error.
|JDT analyzer/planner and diagnostic-isolation acceptance. Implemented-and-validated `001` proves one exact non-recovered target binding and every managed edit site binding for the complete-`AVAILABLE` fixture. Implemented-and-validated `002` proves 12 exact `BOUND_TARGET`, one exact `BOUND_OTHER`, and zero `UNRESOLVED` JDT records while keeping one Java non-code residual occurrence and two non-Java residual paths outside managed edits. Existing `006`/`007`/`009` evidence remains scoped; implemented `008` owns only its four uncertainty cases with closure precedence; and `011` owns ordinary selected-leaf lookup protection.
|bounded binding-clean, candidate-total, six-case negative, and ordinary sidecar-free evidence implemented; broader/general acceptance planned P0

|RPK-JAVA-MOVE-004
|Explicit managed-write eligibility revalidates under lock, before WAL, the full descriptor/edge-prune proof, global candidate exclusion, before/staged authority closure, selected-missing-entry repository identity/paths and negative presence, source/candidate inventory, exact bindings/lookup protection, platform/provider, staged post-image, retained diagnostics/non-concealment, delta, conflicts, and freshness. Any selector ambiguity, missing included descriptor, target uncertainty, new/changed error, artifact appearance/disappearance, or drift refuses without mutation. Caller classpath attestation, Maven/plugin execution, settings credentials, implicit network, and lexical evidence supply no authority.
|Core `PatchEngine` and integration acceptance. Implemented-and-validated `001` proves one lock-held exact staged-image commit and exact rollback after revalidation. Implemented-and-validated `002` independently proves one exact staged-image transaction and exact full non-engine workspace rollback for its observer-discrimination request. Implemented-and-validated `004` proves lexical compatibility-plan core refusal before lock/WAL plus no managed/client application through its bounded surface paths. Existing `005`/`006`/`007`/`009`-`012` evidence remains scoped. Implemented `008` adds one generic immutable `OperationAuthorityFileEvidence` record for the non-managed `BOUND_OTHER` source; one under-lock engine observation returns primary `authorityLease.evidenceDrift` plus secondary `snapshot.scopeChanged` before WAL, preserving `S1`. It reports expected semantic candidate-inventory identity and expected/observed required-file-evidence identities only.

REQ-008 contributes four pre-lock/no-residue guidance cases, one pre-lock/no-residue active-reactor-POM structural refusal, and one lock-held/pre-WAL required-file-evidence refusal. The exact Magrathea `a48e3c06` request remains separately CLI/Linux/JDK-21-qualified; four-host native/general authority remains open.

=== Implemented Local Packaged-process Validation of Existing REQ-001

Status: implementation-informed for one official final OpenJDK 21.0.11 local execution on Linux `amd64` against the mutable, uncommitted tree, with independent review receipts retained separately. Native CI wiring and `if: always()` report uploads are implemented but unexecuted. Linux x86-64 CI, Windows x86-64, macOS x86-64, and macOS arm64 remain `CONFIGURED_UNOBSERVED` until exact post-commit CI receipts are reviewed; Windows/macOS behavior is configured and statically reviewed only.

This is not a thirteenth Maven requirement or a twenty-third case. The dedicated `packagedMavenMoveClassAuthorityTest` source set/task reuses `features/java-maven-move-class-apply-authority.feature`, selects the repository-unique `@REQ-JAVA-MAVEN-MOVE-AUTH-001` pickle, uses packaged-only glue, and emits dedicated Cucumber JSON and JUnit XML. It depends on `refactorkitRuntimeDist`, uses one fork, and is explicitly invoked after packaging. Normal `test` and `check` remain packaging-independent. No external protocol or product semantic changed.

The bounded evidence scope is one no-follow byte copy of the permanent 20-module fixture and the real self-contained unpacked CLI. Subject processes use isolated home/temp directories and a scrubbed allowlisted environment; `JAVA_HOME`, `JDK_JAVA_OPTIONS`, `_JAVA_OPTIONS`, `CLASSPATH`, Maven/Gradle options, and proxy variables are absent, while one controlled `JAVA_TOOL_OPTIONS` supplies isolated home/temp properties. A poison `java` shim is first on minimal `PATH` and remains uninvoked. Launcher bytes and canonical child `java.home` attest the embedded runtime. Observed SHA-256 identities are fixture/copy manifest `905b354c151e8219d6f755335ecac381a0dab83df20de55d22f8a15a8695829e`, unchanged package manifest `681822f781379f5ddbe67389d1e33606f8931ca6fc4a75a3b3fc09f657b88430`, launcher `fa9cd72b54f269d76053da247665cb931ff39631d1ac38c7b55aaa0c9dcadef3`, and embedded Java `bd12685bb6403175bb8803ef143738bf74d06565223be944de1dad20ef5e5625`.

The 16-command sequence observes clean 20-module/seven-source baseline scan/diagnostics, references, definition, repeat scan, two equal `SEMANTIC_PREVIEW` / `JDT_BINDING` / `ELIGIBLE` previews, a contended-lock apply probe, one successful apply, post-apply scan/diagnostics, normal rollback, and restored scan/diagnostics. The exact observer source sets and paths are:

* `catalog-pricing:main` — `catalog-pricing/src/main/java/com/acme/catalog/pricing/CatalogPrice.java`;
* `catalog-storefront:main` — `catalog-storefront/src/main/java/com/acme/catalog/storefront/ProductTile.java`;
* `catalog-acceptance:test` — `catalog-acceptance/src/test/java/com/acme/catalog/acceptance/ProductLifecycleSteps.java`.

The exact five affected paths are those three plus `catalog-model/src/main/java/com/acme/catalog/legacy/Product.java` and `catalog-model/src/main/java/com/acme/catalog/api/Product.java`. The sole schema-v8 transaction contains five top-level `FileEdit` entries: four `Modify` plus one `Rename`. The modifies are the old target path, acceptance steps, pricing source, and storefront source; their nested `TextEdit` counts are exactly `1/2/1/1`, five total. There is no one-edit-per-modify invariant.

The lock probe observes only exact CLI human text `Apply refused:` followed by `ERROR: Workspace is locked by another RefactorKit writer` and refuses before WAL. `workspace.locked` is not projected, and this runner makes no core-code correlation claim. The successful CLI apply replans inside its own child process, compares the rendered preview portion, and makes no cross-process plan-ID claim. The sole transaction reaches `APPLIED`, fresh post-apply diagnostics are clean, normal rollback advances that same record to `ROLLED_BACK`, and exact baseline bytes, path kinds, seven-source inventory, and snapshot identity are restored. The permanent fixture and unpacked package remain unchanged. All 16 commands complete with zero timeouts, truncations, or process leaks.

Official final local packaged evidence used `:modules:refactorkit-cli:packagedMavenMoveClassAuthorityTest --rerun-tasks`: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL` in 22s with 35 tasks. === LATE REQ-004 Implementation Evidence

Status: implementation-informed and reconciled with scenario-level `@implemented-and-validated`.

The corrected feature now uses the returned non-capability `operationId` for known daemon/LSP/MCP refusal and keeps arbitrary `planId=plan-lexical` as unknown input. Current source implements exactly-one non-blank `operationId`/`planId` parsing in core. A cached operation returns typed `evidence.insufficient`; no/both/blank, unknown/evicted operation, and arbitrary plan identities return `INVALID_PARAMS`. Daemon and LSP preserve blocker/envelope in JSON-RPC error `data`; MCP returns equivalent `isError=true` structured content.

The schema-v1 envelope is immutable and reports `NOT_SEMANTICALLY_PROVEN`, `REVIEW_ONLY`, `LEXICAL_FALLBACK`, `INELIGIBLE`, and `evidence.insufficient`. It binds normalized request, exact snapshot, hidden review artifact, and sorted evidence with four SHA-256 identities and deterministic `lexical-review-<sha256>` correlation. The exposed residual records are path/content-hash/risk identities with `managedEdit=false`, not source ranges or token hashes. Only `reviewArtifactSha256` commits to exact internal compatibility edit ranges and replacement/create bytes. Record bounds are 200 and 262,144 canonical UTF-8 bytes, with `COMPLETE or TRUNCATED`, total/returned counts, deterministic returned-risk-category counts, and four fixed next actions.

The direct API under test is `JavaMoveClassOperationDispatcher.preview` and its `JavaMoveClassOperationOutcome.LexicalReview`, not generic `LanguageAdapter.applyRefactoring`. CLI, recipe, daemon session, managed LSP session, and MCP session route Java `moveClass` through that boundary. No envelope exposes a plan/edit/range/replacement/apply token; no pending plan or LSP edit is created; recipe returns no aggregate; the bounded envelope-only LRU cache is lifecycle-cleared; compatibility-plan core apply refuses before lock/WAL; and a fresh clean JDT scan retains existing `SEMANTIC_PREVIEW`/`JDT_BINDING`/`ELIGIBLE` plan behavior.

The dedicated OpenJDK-21 Cucumber report discovered 22 cases, selected/executed/passed the single REQ-004 production case with 38 expanded steps, skipped 21 cases by tag filter, and recorded zero failures/errors. The reviewed focused log SHA-256 is `5fb89c290bd098f249c2ec734c8c7bbffae162e5357ab6b1683042fc68e698ce`; the final dry-run also discovered 22, selected one, skipped 21, and had zero failures/errors, with reviewed log SHA-256 `40814182128d54c599522e84887c2fd8b51c422988daade149dd2b49b83a0a39`. This proves the source-built/in-process scenario only and does not qualify packaged executables, stdio/editor deployment, runtime distributions, native platforms, or general move authority.

REQ-JAVA-MAVEN-MOVE-AUTH-001 through `012` occur in the feature with repository-unique IDs. Ten ordinary scenarios and both six-row outlines (`008`, `012`) carry explicit `@implemented-and-validated`; their example rows inherit one ID, classification, and status. The feature reconciles to twelve definitions and 22 validated cases.

== Pending-plan Store Extraction

Status: implementation-informed and reconciled with feature-level `@implemented-and-validated`. The extraction is a supporting quality/safety contract for existing daemon/LSP/MCP use cases, not a new externally invocable use case.

[cols="2,3,2,2,2",options="header"]
|===
|Requirement ID |Title / acceptance boundary |Classification |Architecture mapping |Declared status and observed evidence

|REQ-PENDING-PLAN-STORE-001
|Exact opaque payloads are retained by reference in separate session-owned generic stores. The fixed capacity is 128; successful lookup refreshes access recency; inserting entry 129 after touching entry 1 evicts entry 2; missing or evicted IDs preserve each surface's existing not-found response and require a new preview. Eviction, remove, and clear affect in-memory references only and create no workspace edit, filesystem write, workspace lock, WAL record, managed transaction, or rollback evidence.
|Non-functional. The scenario constrains bounded resources, isolation, deterministic eviction, exact retention, safety, and absence of write side effects.
|`C-TECH-028`; `QS-PROTO-003`; UC-DAEMON, UC-LSP, and UC-MCP; ADRs 0002 and 0004.
|Feature-level inherited `@implemented-and-validated`; one focused source-built/in-process scenario with 13 steps passed on OpenJDK 21.0.11.

|REQ-PENDING-PLAN-STORE-002
|The shared store exposes only insert, successful access-order lookup, remove, and clear. `DaemonSession`, `LspSession`, and `McpSession` each own a separate instance while retaining their exact asymmetric preview/import admission, discard availability, selected-versus-all apply consumption, refusal consumption/retry, rollback, document/project reset, close, and success/error response policies at surface call sites. The extraction invents no uniform lifecycle, protocol response, approval, or authority rule.
|Non-functional. The scenario constrains compatibility, separation of concerns, protocol stability, and authority/lifecycle ownership.
|`C-TECH-028`; `QS-PROTO-003`; UC-DAEMON, UC-LSP, and UC-MCP; ADRs 0002 and 0004.
|Feature-level inherited `@implemented-and-validated`; one focused source-built/in-process scenario with 6 steps passed on OpenJDK 21.0.11.
|===

Production evidence is exact and bounded to `modules/refactorkit-core/src/main/kotlin/org/refactorkit/core/PendingPlanStore.kt`, `modules/refactorkit-daemon/src/main/kotlin/org/refactorkit/daemon/DaemonSession.kt`, `modules/refactorkit-lsp/src/main/kotlin/org/refactorkit/lsp/LspSession.kt`, and `modules/refactorkit-mcp/src/main/kotlin/org/refactorkit/mcp/McpSession.kt`. The CLI-hosted `PendingPlanStoreCucumberTest` and steps call the core primitive and real session classes in process. The OpenJDK 21.0.11 focused run passed two scenarios/19 steps, executed 29 Gradle tasks, and reported zero failures/errors; log SHA-256 `724e1e1378219cb9f3211ade826c037edb1e31c08c688ea7142a6dc00cca4a6c`. Subsequent independent whole-working-tree verification on 2026-07-31 used OpenJDK 21.0.11 and `./gradlew --no-daemon --rerun-tasks check goldenTest`: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL` in 7m 23s; 1002 discovered, 782 executed, 220 skipped/tag-filtered, 0 failures, 0 errors; 77 tasks executed; log SHA-256 `03848917d90be1c4d3b4852038bd4364d3d9dc9329c3e749f67e44a0828dcdd4`. SpotBugs retained its configured non-blocking baseline and reported no `PendingPlanStore` findings. This is dated mutable source-built aggregate evidence, not packaged/concurrency/general-extraction or numerical coverage qualification.

This evidence does not qualify packaged executables/transports, concurrent access, long-running service races, or a general service-extraction pattern. It does not change protocol success/error shapes, `PlanId` authority, explicit-apply approval, `PatchEngine` gates, or the separate surface lifecycle matrix.

== Managed Rollback Executor Extraction

Status: implementation-informed. REQ-MANAGED-ROLLBACK-EXECUTOR-001/002 inherit `@non-functional-requirement` and `@implemented-and-validated` from `features/managed-rollback-executor-extraction.feature`. One ordinary scenario and one seven-row outline produce two definitions, eight cases, 19 declared steps, and 67 expanded case steps. Production evidence is `ManagedRollbackExecutor.kt`; inspected adoption is in CLI/daemon/LSP/MCP; runner/glue evidence is `ManagedRollbackExecutorCucumberTest.kt` and `ManagedRollbackExecutorSteps.kt`. A focused RED proved mutable diagnostic aliasing by mutating the source list after `execute` (log SHA-256 `952026debab60a44de9090b524fba39e90a03de37f72e1b086a1065176fe9724`); `Refused` now copies diagnostics into a detached unmodifiable ordered snapshot. Final independent focused OpenJDK 21.0.11 source-built/in-process acceptance passed 8/8 scenarios and 67/67 expanded steps with 29 tasks and zero failures, errors, skips, undefined, ambiguous, or pending results (log SHA-256 `7e74ca9cf7080c7b8b2c32295cd17bdec193140346c48a08a4534549e85226ea`). The historical three-feature official Gherkin audit found 16 definitions, 32 cases, 587 expanded steps, and zero errors (log SHA-256 `f4f9fd559ef9220c145017a2723fc4b8e888424448a392a11d20c1dc33d379c0`). The preceding four-feature audit records the two managed-apply selector definitions and 11 cases as implemented-and-validated; the official post-promotion five-feature audit adds five implemented-and-validated Workspace Snapshot Composer definitions/eight cases and leaves this managed-rollback evidence boundary separate. The earlier four-feature absent-status audit remains historical pre-promotion evidence. The historical rollback-scoped whole-working-tree verification used the same OpenJDK and `./gradlew --no-daemon --rerun-tasks check goldenTest`: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL` in 7m 5s; 1010 tests discovered, 790 executed, 220 skipped/tag-filtered, zero failures/errors; 77 tasks; log SHA-256 `2e2b6ef136f9e4eaeedab0c7bb4de7705fbc9b116adc09603631c38d26010c96`. Slice-local SpotBugs findings are non-zero but reviewed/non-blocking: the former three `Refused`-diagnostics EI/EI2 findings are eliminated; seven core medium findings remain (five exact `TransactionLogException` identity warnings required by REQ-MANAGED-ROLLBACK-EXECUTOR-002 and two private dependency-injection collaborator-retention warnings), plus 25 glue-only Kotlin/lifecycle/generated warnings (13 NP, 1 RCN, 5 MS, 6 BC). This is dated mutable source-built aggregate evidence. It does not qualify packaged or cross-platform execution, concurrent rollback, crash/restart, general rollback authority, numerical coverage, or altered `PatchEngine` authority.

[cols="2,3,2,2,2",options="header"]
|===
|Requirement ID |Title / acceptance boundary |Classification |Architecture mapping |Declared status and observed evidence

|REQ-MANAGED-ROLLBACK-EXECUTOR-001
|One visible applied record is durably rolled back through the real engine. The implemented executor parses the exact raw ID, performs one pre-lock full-record lookup, applies `APPLIED_ONLY`, invokes one allow guard before the lock, calls public `PatchEngine.rollback` exactly once with the resolved transaction and supplied `NORMAL` mode, and returns the exact applied transaction plus the same complete resolved pre-lock record. `PatchEngine` alone owns its under-lock reload and rollback rules; the executor owns no caller response, lifecycle, refresh, diagnostics, or mode policy.
|Non-functional. The scenario constrains ordering, responsibility boundaries, exact evidence retention, durability handoff, non-duplication, and behavior preservation rather than adding a new user-visible capability.
|`UC-ROLLBACK`; `C-TECH-029`; `C-BOUND-006`; `QS-SAFE-012`; `R-030`; ADRs 0002 and 0004; Chapters 4-6 and 8-12.
|Feature-level inherited `@implemented-and-validated`; the ordinary 11-step definition/case passed in the focused source-built/in-process run.

|REQ-MANAGED-ROLLBACK-EXECUTOR-002
|Short circuits and surface visibility retain distinct outcomes. Seven independent example rows preserve invalid raw input, visible absence, `APPLIED_ONLY` hiding of a non-`APPLIED` record, daemon `JOURNAL_RECORD` engine refusal, LSP dirty-document-first preflight rejection, outer lookup journal failure, and rollback-call journal failure. Each row fixes parse/lookup/guard/engine/lock counts and preserves the exact typed outcome and available failure/resolved-record evidence without moving response, session, refresh, or diagnostics policy into the executor. Exact journal-exception identity is retained; refusal diagnostics retain construction-time order and values in a detached unmodifiable snapshot rather than aliasing a mutable source list.
|Non-functional. The outline constrains cross-surface compatibility, fail-fast ordering, document safety, outcome discrimination, exception fidelity, evidence identity, and separation of concerns.
|`UC-ROLLBACK`; `C-TECH-029`; `C-BOUND-006`; `QS-SAFE-012`; `R-030`; ADRs 0002 and 0004; Chapters 4-6 and 8-12.
|Feature-level inherited `@implemented-and-validated`; the outline's seven cases/eight declared steps/56 expanded case steps passed in the focused source-built/in-process run.
|===

The feature, production executor, acceptance glue, final focused run, and historical rollback-scoped whole-working-tree source-built run provide bounded implementation evidence for `ManagedRollbackExecutor`, `RollbackLookupVisibility`, and the typed outcome algebra. The mutable-diagnostics RED and corrected acceptance additionally prove detached unmodifiable ordered snapshotting for `Refused`; exact `TransactionLogException` identity and the exact resolved record remain intentionally retained. The retained record is explicitly pre-lock lookup evidence rather than current or post-rollback state. CLI/daemon/LSP/MCP adoption is confirmed by source inspection only, not packaged or cross-platform execution. Existing rollback evidence and `PatchEngine` authority remain bounded and unchanged.

== Managed-apply Diagnostics-gate Selector

Status: implementation-informed within a bounded source-built/in-process selector and daemon/MCP regression boundary, with dated whole-working-tree aggregate evidence. `features/managed-apply-diagnostics-gate-selector.feature` contains one ten-row `Scenario Outline` and one ordinary `Scenario`: two repository-unique non-functional requirement definitions, 11 expanded cases, 20 declared scenario steps, and 92 expanded case steps. Production evidence is `ManagedApplyDiagnosticsGateSelector.kt`; source inspection confirms adoption at `DaemonSession.refactorApply`, `McpSession.toolApplyRefactoring`, and the bounded `LspSession.applyPlan` path; runner/glue evidence is `ManagedApplyDiagnosticsGateSelectorCucumberTest.kt` and `ManagedApplyDiagnosticsGateSelectorSteps.kt`, supported by the JVM module build scaffold. Final independent OpenJDK 21.0.11 acceptance passed 11/11 scenarios and 92/92 steps with zero failures, errors, skips, undefined, ambiguous, or pending results (log SHA-256 `74749cf8635d7bbbac3abb4a3eeaf669f6aab3d58770e423094ce791c7882997`). |REQ-WORKSPACE-SNAPSHOT-COMPOSER-005
|Only daemon and MCP adopt the stateless core composer. Daemon invokes it at project open, saved-workspace refresh, apply baseline, apply post-image, and rollback refresh; MCP invokes it at project scan, apply baseline, apply post-image, and rollback refresh. Exact extension mapping stays in surface/adapter wiring. Refresh/saved state, workspace index, pending/authority state, semantic lifecycle, `PatchEngine` authority, and protocol policy remain surface-owned; CLI/LSP, concrete adapter imports in core, a generalized registry/new module, stored state, index, protocol, persistence, and mutation authority remain excluded.
|Non-functional. Separation of concerns, compatibility, dependency direction, least authority, and bounded adoption are architecture quality constraints.
|`UC-SCAN`, `UC-DAEMON`, and `UC-MCP`; `C-TECH-031`; `C-BOUND-008`; `QS-SNAPSHOT-002`; `R-032`; ADRs 0004/0009/0010 decision fit; Chapters 2, 4-10, and 12.
|Feature-level inherited `@implemented-and-validated`; this one eight-step case passed through source inspection of the exact core/daemon/MCP wiring and exclusions, supported by daemon/MCP module regressions. It is not packaged surface execution evidence.
|===

REQ-WORKSPACE-SNAPSHOT-COMPOSER-005 preserves these exact surface-owned exclusions:

[cols="2,5",options="header"]
|===
|Policy boundary |Surface-owned decisions

|Refresh and saved state
|Invocation timing, stored root and snapshot, watcher dirty or overflow state, refresh count, and response selection.

|Workspace index
|Open, reconcile, contribution, generation, and symbol discovery.

|Pending and authority state
|Pending plans, lexical-review audit, admission, removal, clearing, and semantic leases.

|Semantic lifecycle
|Toolchain or adapter discovery, start, stop, restart, reset, close, and post-apply scan-before-cleanup ordering.

|`PatchEngine` authority
|Preview routing, diagnostics gates, authorization, lock, WAL, apply, validation, recovery, and automatic or explicit rollback.

|Protocol policy
|Capabilities, JSON or text rendering, error mapping, changed-file projection, and success or refusal responses.
|===

CLI and LSP retain their existing scan and lifecycle behavior. Core imports no concrete Java, Tree-sitter, TypeScript, Kotlin, daemon, or MCP type and gains no generalized scanner registry or new orchestration module. The composer returns only an immutable snapshot and owns no stored state, index, protocol, persistence, or mutation authority.

The composer production class and focused acceptance directly validate REQ-WORKSPACE-SNAPSHOT-COMPOSER-001 through `004` and the source-inspected ownership assertions of `005`. Independent daemon/MCP regressions validate compatibility at the two adopting modules without turning source inspection into packaged execution evidence. The final dated whole-working-tree source-built aggregate passed with no production finding attributable to the composer or daemon/MCP adoption. Its static result is not zero: 21 slice-local glue-only Kotlin/generated findings remain reviewed/non-blocking and are not production defects. LATE ADR reassessment found no new decision or body update; LATE C4 reassessment validated `docs/c4/workspace.dsl` unchanged because the implementation remains internal C3-scale behavior inside the existing Core Patch and Model Engine and existing in-process relationships.

== Java Refactoring Preview Command Boundary

Status: implementation-informed and reconciled with feature-level `@implemented-and-validated`. `features/java-refactoring-preview-command-boundary.feature` has no `Background`; one five-row `Scenario Outline` and three ordinary scenarios produce four repository-unique non-functional definitions, eight cases, 46 declared scenario steps, and 94 expanded steps. Production evidence is `JavaRefactoringPreviewCommand.kt`: a sealed hierarchy containing only `RenameClass`, `RenameMember`, `MoveSourceRoot`, single-file `OrganizeImports`, and `SafeDelete`; a fieldless `JavaRefactoringPreviewDispatcher`; an internal per-call `JavaRefactoringPreviewPlannerInvoker` seam; and `ExistingJavaRefactoringPreviewPlannerInvoker`, which maps to the unchanged default planners. Five generic `JavaLanguageAdapter` decoders converge on the dispatcher. Daemon and MCP construct only these five commands after their existing parsing/required-field checks; their refused-plan, pending-plan, and rendering policies remain outside.

The pre-implementation baseline passed Java 245/245, daemon 58/58, and MCP 26/26 on OpenJDK 21.0.11 (log SHA-256 `46a52ccf5fb116f5f52c7b116947ade4ff8faceea0d2fa999c330cc5cc4f1560`). The absent API produced a meaningful compile RED (SHA-256 `3221bc7bc6aaef1a21117032ce6f22ea7c28ea8efb05a2797e2fec1beb3b5af8`). The first independent review correctly blocked promotion because REQ-003 was source-only. The correction reused the same feature through split runners: the Java-module runner executes REQ-001/002/004 with sentinel plus real default planners; the CLI-module runner executes REQ-003 through public `DaemonSession`/`McpSession` dispatch, covering all five operations plus missing, unknown, and refused paths and no preview writes/residue. Final focused evidence passed seven Java scenarios/85 steps plus one real-surface scenario/9 steps—four definitions, eight cases, 94/94 steps—with zero failures/errors (log SHA-256 `10c805b67306026cb4ab4a79de9b7258c712e28aaf0bb0070279edbbc44914f5`; JSON SHA-256 `2059c28d57fde8f72b2e3f0de9fe6d95062c8de6cad40aeaeee3e4175a378ef6` and `999ee1a13d8577113113236e7b2241a5fac881e97327705eb1e4d1858ed83baa`). Independent regressions reported Java 253 discovered/252 executed/one tag-filtered, daemon 58/58, and MCP 26/26 with zero failures/errors (log SHA-256 `9a1cb587e7e377682a09353f470140a76e4dae1e5cb42a727f8ed346e2a0e9b1`).

The official post-promotion six-feature Gherkin audit reports 27 definitions/unique IDs, 59 cases, 386 declared steps, and 838 expanded steps, all implemented-and-validated with zero parse/tag/status/duplicate/reconciliation errors (SHA-256 `f20f39f2421edaf4c5208363a3479a0f7696635631a27d95aeb2a99d04d9be0d`). LATE ADR review required no ADR change; LATE C4 validation required no DSL change. External protocol schemas and capability catalogues did not change. `moveClass`, other languages, mixed/experimental/Maven routes, CLI/LSP adoption, multi-file imports, admission/lifecycle, diagnostics, managed writes, and `ManagedApplyInvoker` remain excluded. Focused evidence remains source-built/in-process. Final dated mutable whole-working-tree source-built verification on 2026-08-01 used OpenJDK 21.0.11 and `./gradlew --no-daemon --rerun-tasks check goldenTest`: `BUILD SUCCESSFUL` in 6m 54s; 1045 tests discovered, 817 executed, 228 skipped/tag-filtered, zero failures/errors; 140 XML files; 79 tasks executed; log SHA-256 `fddde07b954c36898252e510ad07758408f2c9b4e35b9102a5e71d7dd518d535`. The targeted affected-module static command passed (log SHA-256 `8f29ec9da3ff6a96d0044423e8ac1f415b2651ce0870fab9b2b86d6f5f007c33`). Static classification is deliberately non-zero: the full targeted log contains no finding owned by `JavaRefactoringPreviewCommand`, `JavaRefactoringPreviewDispatcher`, `ExistingJavaRefactoringPreviewPlannerInvoker`, daemon `refactorPreview`, MCP `toolPreviewRefactoring`, or the five changed generic decoder helpers. Exactly 48 slice-local glue findings remain reviewed/non-blocking: Java-module command glue 24 = 15 NP, 6 RCN, 1 Dm, and 2 BC; CLI real-surface glue 24 = 13 NP, 7 RCN, and 4 BC. Finding text that references dispatcher methods belongs to generated Kotlin nullability analysis at glue call sites, not production methods. The wider configured SpotBugs/PMD baselines remain non-blocking; aggregate static cleanliness and numerical coverage are not claimed. This dated mutable aggregate does not add Gherkin definitions/cases or qualify packaged, native, cross-platform, concurrent, crash-restart, general-orchestration, or numerical-coverage behavior.

== Transactionality Requirement Clarifications

These requirements capture the audit's required contract decisions and acceptance gaps. They are not implementation claims.

[cols="1,4,1",options="header"]
|===
|ID |Requirement |Status

|REQ-TX-001
|Define atomicity, consistency validation, isolation, durability, compensation, rollback, and crash recovery separately; do not describe current preflighted compensatable batches as ACID or crash-safe transactions.
|needs-review; release-blocking terminology

|REQ-TX-002
|Define the transaction boundary for one plan, recipe, native LSP edit, custom managed LSP apply, diagnostics, and direct library use.
|needs-review

|REQ-TX-003
|Cover process kill, power loss, disk full, permission change, non-atomic filesystems, log corruption, concurrent writers, and editor-buffer divergence in the failure model.
|planned acceptance

|REQ-TX-004
|Normal rollback must detect post-apply conflicts and refuse rather than silently overwrite later changes; forced and manual recovery require explicit contracts.
|planned release blocker

|REQ-TX-005
|Define a durable versioned journal lifecycle, integrity and permissions, retention, startup recovery, and observable incomplete-recovery states before managed writes are called crash-safe.
|planned release blocker

|REQ-TX-006
|Define snapshot scope, engine-verified affected-file preconditions, per-path expected hashes, metadata scope, case sensitivity, generated files, and symbolic-link behavior.
|planned release blocker

|REQ-TX-007
|Define authoritative diagnostics and regression gates before, during simulation, after apply, and after rollback, including deterministic recovery when validation fails.
|planned release blocker

|REQ-TX-008
|Define one-writer workspace locking, timeout, stale-lock recovery, cancellation, and multi-process behavior.
|planned release blocker

|REQ-TX-009
|Classify native LSP edits as client-managed/no-rollback, overlay full-sync buffers, require monotonic versions, emit exact open-document versions, refuse incapable clients, and guard managed disk writes from open-buffer divergence.
|implemented and tested (`TX-007` closed)

|REQ-TX-010
|Stage every recipe step against one evolving immutable snapshot, expose one recipe-wide plan, and perform at most one managed WAL transaction; planning failure and no-op execution must not write journal records.
|implemented and tested (`TX-008` closed)

|REQ-TX-011
|Use one coordinate space per file/plan, merge all same-file edits, validate complete line/character bounds and cross-edit overlap, and render every final file before mutation.
|planned release blocker

|REQ-TX-012
|Treat explicit managed apply as auditable approval, refuse missing authorization, persist approval identity, map managed refusal categories deterministically, and refresh integration state after mutation.
|implemented and tested (`TX-016`, `TX-017`, `TX-018` closed)
|===

The independent receipt classified the slice as reviewed/non-blocking: the five corrected module-rename contract target classes have zero EI/EI2; the authoritative-evaluation EI is non-real on a defensive unmodifiable diagnostics list; relevant BC/Bx reports are Kotlin compiler collection-cast/non-correctness-boxing artifacts; and the 47 authoritative glue findings (27 BC, 11 MS, 6 NP, 1 RCN, 2 UrF) plus 113 module-rename glue findings (37 BC, 19 MS, 46 NP, 10 RCN, 1 REC) are test/glue lifecycle-nullability or generated-bytecode/static-field reports. The Maven move-class feature's `Business Need` addresses `UC-JAVA-MOVE-CLASS` for a multi-module Maven workspace maintainer. Its `Background` is not a production scenario. Each of twelve requirement-bearing definitions (ten `Scenario`, two `Scenario Outline`) has one repository-unique stable `@REQ-*` ID, at least one classification, and exactly one effective status. All twelve are `@implemented-and-validated`; the six rows under each outline inherit its ID/classification/status, producing 22 validated executable cases. Those execution totals do not alter the Maven subset and do not imply packaged-process/runtime/native-platform/general authority or numerical coverage qualification; the SpotBugs baseline remains configured non-blocking. === REQ-MANAGED-ROLLBACK-EXECUTOR-002 Validated Example Matrix

All seven rows inherit REQ-MANAGED-ROLLBACK-EXECUTOR-002, `@non-functional-requirement`, and `@implemented-and-validated` from one repository-unique `Scenario Outline`. The focused source-built/in-process runner passed every row; the work counts and typed outcomes below are bounded acceptance evidence, while named surface adoption is source-inspected.

[cols="1,2,3,3",options="header"]
|===
|Example / surface |Initial condition and visibility |Validated work boundary |Validated typed outcome and evidence

|1 — any rollback surface
|Upper-case raw transaction ID; journal must not be accessed; caller-selected visibility; guard must not run.
|`parseOrNull=1`; outer `loadRecord=0`; guard=0; real rollback=0; workspace lock=0.
|`InvalidTransactionId` carries the exact unnormalized raw ID; no parsed ID or resolved record.

|2 — CLI
|No ID-matching record; `APPLIED_ONLY`; guard must not run.
|Parse once; one outer pre-lock lookup; no guard, rollback, or lock.
|`TransactionNotFound` carries the parsed ID and `APPLIED_ONLY`; no resolved record.

|3 — CLI, LSP, and MCP
|One complete ID-matching `ROLLED_BACK` record; `APPLIED_ONLY`; guard must not run.
|Parse and outer lookup once; visibility hides the record; no guard, rollback, or lock.
|`TransactionNotFound` carries the parsed ID and `APPLIED_ONLY`; hidden non-`APPLIED` record is not exposed.

|4 — daemon
|One complete ID-matching `ROLLED_BACK` record; `JOURNAL_RECORD`; allow guard.
|Parse, outer lookup, guard, and real rollback once; engine lock reached.
|`Refused` carries the exact resolved record and a detached unmodifiable snapshot of the ordered `transaction.notApplied` baseline; mutating the retained source list after `execute` changes the source but not the outcome, and mutation through the outcome list is rejected. The executor neither reloads nor reconstructs the record.

|5 — LSP
|One complete `APPLIED` record; `APPLIED_ONLY`; unrelated dirty document and affected open path both exist.
|Parse, lookup, visibility, and one pre-lock guard; dirty-document-first rejection; no rollback or lock.
|`PreflightRejected` carries the same resolved record and unchanged `DOCUMENT_VERSION_MISMATCH` payload.

|6 — daemon lookup
|Outer `loadRecord` throws exact `TransactionLogException E_lookup`; `JOURNAL_RECORD`; guard must not run.
|Parse and one outer pre-lock lookup; no guard, rollback, or lock.
|`JournalLookupFailed` carries parsed ID and exact code/message/cause; no resolved record.

|7 — daemon rollback call
|Outer lookup resolves one complete `APPLIED` record; under-lock engine journal access throws exact `TransactionLogException E_call`; `JOURNAL_RECORD`; allow guard.
|Parse, outer lookup, guard, real rollback, and engine lock each reached once.
|`RollbackCallJournalFailed` carries the same resolved record and exact code/message/cause.
|===

Visible absence, preflight rejection, engine refusal, lookup journal failure, and rollback-call journal failure remain distinct. Every row excludes response projection, session lifecycle, refresh, and diagnostics policy from the executor.

== Validation Modes

[cols="1,3",options="header"]
|===
|Mode |Meaning

|Documentation evidence
|Behavior is described in repository docs or `AGENTS.md`.

|Source evidence
|Behavior appears in module source files.

|Unit test evidence
|Behavior has module tests.

|Golden test evidence
|Behavior has a `testdata/golden` case.

|CI evidence
|Behavior is exercised by `.github/workflows/ci.yml`.

|Domain research
|A reviewed requirement or concept informs planned architecture but is not repository implementation or executable evidence.

|Field report
|A dated external observation motivates priority and qualification; it does not replace a permanent fixture or prove general support.

|Gherkin source reconciliation
|Feature discovery, stable IDs, classifications, effective statuses, paths, totals, and architecture/use-case mappings agree with the `.feature` source. This validates documentation consistency only, not behavior or implementation.

|Tagged Cucumber evidence
|A runner filtered by a stable requirement tag executes the selected production scenario. Passing steps support only that scenario and exercised surface; scenarios reported skipped because of the filter remain unvalidated.

|Packaged-process Cucumber evidence
|A dedicated runner executes an existing tagged pickle through the real self-contained launcher and embedded runtime as child OS processes. It is a validation mode, not a new requirement/case. The REQ-001 mode is implementation-informed from one local Linux `amd64` mutable-tree observation; each post-commit native CI row remains independently `CONFIGURED_UNOBSERVED` until its exact reviewed receipt exists. Source-built, generic package, static-review, or local evidence cannot promote those ledger rows.
|===
